function	script	Promotional_Staff	{
	mes "[宣传员]";
	mes "我们正在寻找充满好奇心、极其勇敢的冒险者。";
	mes "加入我们，开启精彩的冒险吧！";
	next;
	mes "[宣传员]";
	mes "嘿，你是个见多识广的人，对吧？对我的故事感兴趣吗？";
	next;
	switch(select("是的。:不用了，谢谢。")) {
	case 1:
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "你是真正的冒险者。";
		mes "很好！";
		mes "你不会后悔的。";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "我通常会派冒险者前往";
		mes "新发现的地方进行研究。";
		mes "这非常有挑战性，因为从未有人去过那些地方。";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "任务相当危险，";
		mes "所以只有勇敢的人";
		mes "才有资格接受";
		mes "这项任务的挑战。";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "我不确定你的实力是否足够，但你看起来很勇敢。去找王国接待员怎么样？他应该在普隆德拉城堡的第一个房间。";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "根据我听说的情况……那里不是一块新大陆。";
		mes "我不知道它在哪里。";
		mes "嗯……在天空里面？";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "也许只是传闻，";
		mes "但我也不确定。";
		mes "这不是我负责的事。";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "总之，我要把这件事告诉许多冒险者。";
		mes "没时间浪费了！";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "哼，我还以为你是真正的冒险者。你错过了大好机会。你一点也不勇敢，绝对不是！";
		close;
	}
}

prontera,163,53,3	script	Promotional Staff#prt	100,{
	callfunc "Promotional_Staff";
	end;
}

-	script	Recruiter for the Brave_	-1,{
	if (ep13_ryu == 12) {
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "你怎么又来了？";
		mes "你最好去施瓦茨瓦尔德登记。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "什么？你来这里是要传达消息？说吧。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "到旁边去。有一位管理者负责我们所有人，也负责米德加尔特的国际关系。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "直接和他谈吧。";
		close;
	}
	if (ep13_ryu > 8) {
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "去那里吧。";
		mes "你从我这里什么也得不到。";
		mes "赶快前往";
		mes "^FF0000莱岑^000000，三大王国的发源地……";
		close;
	}
	if (ep13_ryu == 8) {
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "我听说过你。";
		mes "你已经通过宣传员的测试了吗？";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "你绝对足够勇敢，可以前往这片新土地。";
		mes "现在我任命你去探索并在灰烬真空中冒险。";
		next;
		select("灰烬真空？");
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "嗯，那是你将要冒险之地的代号。坦白说，我们并不确切知道那里是什么，但我们称它为‘灰烬真空’。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "信不信由你，";
		mes "这绝对是真的。";
		mes "所以请仔细听。";
		mes "拜托了。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "灰烬真空……是一处生机之地……与我们现在生活的地方完全不同。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "那是未知之地。";
		mes "我们不知道那里有什么，";
		mes "也不知道谁生活在那里。";
		mes "那超出了我们的想象。";
		mes "没人知道。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "只有像你一样勇敢的人才值得去那里。你身边会有许多危险，但你必须代表米德加尔特探索那里。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "嗯……";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "总之，我确信那里有许多名利双收的机会……";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "首先，你应该前往 ^FF0000莱岑^000000 登记加入米德加尔特联合联盟。我想他们正在雷根贝勒公司总部开会……";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "在那里你能得到比我更详细的指引。去找他吧……";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "我能做的都做了。去那里开始行动吧！";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "记住，前往 ^FF0000莱岑^000000，然后按照那里的指引行动。";
		next;
		changequest 10064,10065;
		set ep13_ryu,9;
		if (checkre(3))
			getexp 66000,21000;
		else
			getexp 660000,210000;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "去那里吧！";
		mes "仔细听清楚";
		mes "你的任务内容！";
		mes "我说完了……";
		close;
	}
	if (ep13_ryu > 0) {
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "你在这里做什么？";
		mes "直接去 ^FF0000艾尔帕兰^000000 找 ^FF0000宣传员^000000。按我说的做，别浪费时间。";
		close;
	}
	mes "[招募者]";
	mes "勇敢的人们！到我这里来！";
	mes "真正勇敢的人！";
	mes "愿意尝试具有挑战性的任务吗？";
	next;
	mes "[招募者]";
	mes "我是普隆德拉寻找勇者的招募者！我的目标是为这项任务招募最勇敢的冒险者。";
	next;
	mes "[招募者]";
	mes "你愿意加入吗？";
	mes "这里随时欢迎你。";
	mes "我知道，你充满好奇。";
	mes "想尝试什么就去尝试吧。";
	next;
	switch(select("是的，我感兴趣。:不用了，谢谢。")) {
	case 1:
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "哦！是的！你真好奇！";
		mes "你是真正的冒险者。";
		mes "拥有真正的冒险热情。";
		mes "好！让我先告诉你任务的故事！！";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "这些事宣传员当然也能告诉你，但他们不太可靠。所以让我来解释。仔细听就好。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "嗯……其实没什么好说的……";
		mes "...";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "重点是，我们需要一个既好奇又勇敢的人前往新地方。而我选择了你！！";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "这是未知之地中的特殊之处，非常特别！现在我不能告诉你太多。相信我，它真的非同寻常！";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "那么现在，让我来测试你。";
		mes "等你合格之后，";
		mes "我会把一切告诉你。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "去艾尔帕兰的 ^FF0000宣传员^000000 那里取得许可。他们比我更能仔细评估你。去吧。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "只要你说是普隆德拉的招募者派来的，他们就会处理一切。";
		next;
		setquest 10057;
		set ep13_ryu,1;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "时间就是 Zeny，快去吧。";
		mes "一旦拜访宣传员，你就能获准前往未知之地。";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "你真的在享受冒险吗？看来你没那么好奇。";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "去做你自己的事吧！";
		next;
		mes "[招募者]";
		mes "-嘟囔与抱怨……-";
		close;
	}
}

aldebaran,127,138,3	script	Promotional Staff#alde	100,{
	if (ep13_ryu > 3) {
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "你参加测试了吗，还是已经在享受冒险了？";
		close;
	}
	if (ep13_ryu == 3) {
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "去拜访 ^FF0000吉芬的宣传员^000000 吧。你没必要再来找我了。祝福你，愿你冒险平安。";
		close;
	}
	if (ep13_ryu == 2) {
		if (countitem(909) < 300) {
			mes "[宣传员]";
			mes "别忘了带来……^FF0000300 个杰勒比结晶^000000。";
			mes "明白了吗？^FF0000300 个杰勒比结晶^000000！";
			mes "收集齐后告诉我。";
			close;
		}
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "嘿！收集齐后告诉我。";
		mes "别再打扰我了。我很忙……真浪费时间。";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "嗯？你把它们全带来了吗？";
		mes "不！这真是件令人烦恼的工作。";
		mes "没想到你竟然能完成带来 300 个杰勒比结晶这种琐事！";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "你真有胆量，小子！";
		mes "你是个意志坚定的人！";
		mes "好，你赢了！";
		mes "你通过了！！";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "像你这样的冒险者";
		mes "可不常见。";
		mes "真是身经百战的战士！";
		mes "比我还强！";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "现在你能去找";
		mes "吉芬的宣传员吗？";
		mes "他会再次测试你。";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "你知道这不是简单的任务。";
		mes "你已经决心完成它了，对吧？";
		mes "做好心理准备吧。";
		next;
		delitem 909,300; //Jellopy
		changequest 10058,10059;
		set ep13_ryu,3;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "总之，你完成了第一步。";
		mes "祝福你！";
		close;
	}
	if (ep13_ryu == 1) {
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "我们需要充满好奇心、极其勇敢的冒险者。加入我们，开启精彩的冒险吧！";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "嘿，你是个见多识广的人，对吧？对我的故事感兴趣吗？";
		next;
		select("我有东西给你。");
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "给我的？？";
		mes "我讨厌麻烦事。";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "啊……我就知道你会提出请求。";
		mes "是招募者派你来接受测试的吗？";
		mes "嗯。";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "啊！他们总是逼我，";
		mes "让我承受这么多工作，";
		mes "我的日子真难过！";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "我的工作就是测试你是否足够强大、勇敢，能否胜任这次冒险。";
		mes "真麻烦！";
		next;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "如果你带来 ^FF0000300 个杰勒比结晶^000000，我就认可你。";
		mes "我相信你能把它们带给我。";
		mes "准备好了吗？";
		next;
		changequest 10057,10058;
		set ep13_ryu,2;
		mes "[宣传员]";
		mes "去打怪或做其他事情，收集 300 个杰勒比结晶吧。";
		mes "这样我们就能继续前进。";
		close;
	}
	callfunc "Promotional_Staff";
	end;
}

function	script	Guard_13_1	{
	mes "[Guard]";
	if (ep13_ryu > 19) {
		mes "不许进入。你们都被要求前往卢恩·米德加尔特的次元时间缝隙！";
		next;
		mes "[Guard]";
		mes "去那里吧。这是限制区域。";
		close2;
		warp "lhz_in01",getarg(1),getarg(2);
		end;
	}
	if (ep13_ryu > 8) {
		mes "你是来登记成为三王国成员的吗？";
		next;
		mes "[Guard]";
		mes "进去吧。";
		close;
	}
	else {
		mes "不许进入。";
		if (!getarg(0)) close;
		close2;
		warp "lhz_in01",getarg(1),getarg(2);
		end;
	}
	end;
}

moc_fild22b,1,1,0	script	Head of the Alliance#moo	-1,{
OnInit:
	disablenpc "Head of the Alliance#moo";
	end;

OnEnable:
	mapannounce "moc_fild22b","Head of the Alliance: I command that all soldiers attack the adventurers around here for 15 minutes. This is an order!",bc_map;
	enablenpc "Head of the Alliance#moo";
	set $@ep13_test,1;
	initnpctimer;
	monster "moc_fild22b",0,0,"Allied Soldier",1851,80,"Head of the Alliance#moo::OnMyMobDead";
	end;

OnReset:
	killmonster "moc_fild22b","Head of the Alliance#moo::OnMyMobDead";
	end;

OnDisable:
	killmonster "moc_fild22b","Head of the Alliance#moo::OnMyMobDead";
	mapannounce "moc_fild22b","Head of the Alliance: All alliance members. All of you should stop your attacks and return to work. That's all.",bc_map;
	set $@ep13_test,0;
	disablenpc "Head of the Alliance#moo";
	end;

OnMyMobDead:
	if (mobcount("moc_fild22b","Head of the Alliance#moo::OnMyMobDead") < 41) {
		donpcevent "Head of the Alliance#moo::OnDisable";
	}
	end;

OnTimer900000:
	killmonster "moc_fild22b","Head of the Alliance#moo::OnMyMobDead";
	mapannounce "moc_fild22b","Head of the Alliance: All alliance members. All of you should stop your attacks and return to work. That's all.",bc_map;
	set $@ep13_test,2;
	disablenpc "Head of the Alliance#moo";
	stopnpctimer;
	end;
}

	}
}

OnInit:
	enablenpc "Research Official#ep131";
	end;

OnEnable:
	hideoffnpc "Research Official#ep131";
	end;

OnDisable:
	hideonnpc "Research Official#ep131";
	end;

OnMeet:
	donpcevent "Ryosen#ep131_rhea05::OnEnable";
	donpcevent "Hue#ep131_rhea06::OnEnable";
	donpcevent "Hansenne#ep131_rhea07::OnEnable";
	end;

OnCall:
	initnpctimer;
	monster "mid_campin",376,134,"盗虫",1051,1,"Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead";
	monster "mid_campin",376,134,"盗虫",1051,1,"Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead";
	monster "mid_campin",376,134,"盗虫",1051,1,"Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead";
	monster "mid_campin",376,134,"盗虫",1051,1,"Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead";
	monster "mid_campin",376,134,"盗虫",1051,1,"Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead";
	monster "mid_campin",376,134,"盗虫",1051,1,"Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead";
	monster "mid_campin",376,134,"盗虫",1051,1,"Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead";
	monster "mid_campin",376,134,"盗虫",1051,1,"Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead";
	monster "mid_campin",376,134,"盗虫",1051,1,"Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead";
	monster "mid_campin",376,134,"盗虫",1051,1,"Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead";
	donpcevent "Research Official#ep131::OnDisable";
	donpcevent "Ryosen#ep131_rhea05::OnDisable";
	donpcevent "Hue#ep131_rhea06::OnDisable";
	donpcevent "Hansenne#ep131_rhea07::OnDisable";
	end;

OnMyMobDead:
	if (mobcount("mid_campin","Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead") < 1) {
		donpcevent "Research Official#ep131::OnEnable";
		stopnpctimer;
	}
	end;

OnTimer300000:
	killmonster "mid_campin","Research Official#ep131::OnMyMobDead";
	donpcevent "Research Official#ep131::OnEnable";
	stopnpctimer;
	end;
}

// Part Time Work :: ep13_alba

mid_camp,1,1,0	script	#timer_alba01	844,{
	callfunc "F_GM_NPC";
	mes "请输入密码";
	next;
	if (callfunc("F_GM_NPC",1854,0) < 1) {
		mes "密码错误。";
		close;
	} else {
	mes "当前状态：";
		if ($@PartTimeOn == 1) {
		mes "招募中。";
		}
		else {
		mes "未招募。";
		}
	mes "已招募 " + $@PartTimeSlots + " 名兼职人员。";
	mes "你想做什么？";
		next;
		switch(select("Reset the recruiting.:Cancel.")) {
		case 1:
		mes "招募已重置。";
		mes "计时器已停止！";
			stopnpctimer;
			next;
		mes "全局值已重置。";
			set $@PartTimeOn,0;
			set $@PartTimeSlots,0;
			next;
		mes "招募已重置。";
		mes "计时器已启动！";
			donpcevent "#timer_alba01::OnEnable";
			close;
		case 2:
		mes "已取消。";
			close;
		}
	}

OnInit:
	set $@PartTimeOn,0;
	set $@PartTimeSlots,0;
OnEnable:
	initnpctimer;
	end;

OnStop:
	stopnpctimer;
	end;

OnTimer1000:
	set $@PartTimeSlots,0;
	set $@PartTimeOn,1;
	mapannounce "mid_camp", "饲育员塔布：营地的冒险者请注意！我的饲育场招募5名兼职，先到者先得！",bc_map,"0x00ff00";
	end;

OnTimer180000:
OnTimer360000:
	if ($@PartTimeOn < 5) mapannounce "mid_camp", "饲育员塔布：我在饲育场招募兼职，有兴趣请来农场。",bc_map,"0x00ff00";
	else mapannounce "mid_camp", "饲育员塔布：饲育场招募结束。下次见，谢谢！",bc_map,"0x00ff00";
	end;

OnTimer600000:
	mapannounce "mid_camp", "饲育员塔布：饲育场招募结束。下次见，谢谢！",bc_map,"0x00ff00";
	set $@PartTimeOn,0;
	end;

OnTimer7800000:
	donpcevent "#timer_alba01::OnEnable";
	donpcevent "#monster_master::OnEnable";
	end;
}

		mes "以及30,000 JEXP。^000000";

			}

			}
		}
		else if (ep13_alba == 4) {
			if ((countitem(517) > 49) && (countitem(537) > 29)) {
				mes "哦，谢谢！你带来了它们的食物！来得正好，它们准备大吃一顿了。山羊狮一定喜欢。";

				mes "以及3,000 JEXP。^000000";
				} else {
					getexp 80000,30000;

				mes "以及30,000 JEXP。^000000";

			}

				mes "[Taab]";
				mes "别忘了50份肉和30份宠物食品。这些是给山羊狮的。";
				close;
			}
		}
		else if (ep13_alba == 5) {

		close;
	}
}

mid_camp,1,2,0	script	#monster_master	844,{
OnEnable:
	if (rand(1,3) > 1) initnpctimer;
	end;

OnStop:
	killmonster "mid_camp","#monster_master::OnMyMobDead";
	stopnpctimer;
	end;

OnTimer3600000:
	set .@monster,rand(1,3);
	if (.@monster == 1) {
		monster "mid_camp",149,291,"Escaped Tatacho",1986,1,"#monster_master::OnMyMobDead";
		mapannounce "mid_camp", "Breeder Taab: Argh! My Tatacho ran away!",bc_map,"0x00ff00";
		donpcevent "Tatacho#alba02::OnDisable";
	}
	else if (.@monster == 2) {
		monster "mid_camp",154,273,"Escaped Hillsrion",1989,1,"#monster_master::OnMyMobDead";
		mapannounce "mid_camp", "Breeder Taab: Argh! My Hillsrion ran away!",bc_map,"0x00ff00";
		donpcevent "Hillsrion#alba01::OnDisable";
	}
	else {
		monster "mid_camp",184,246,"Escaped Cornus",1992,1,"#monster_master::OnMyMobDead";
		mapannounce "mid_camp", "Breeder Taab: Argh! My Cornus ran away!",bc_map,"0x00ff00";
		donpcevent "Cornus#alba03::OnDisable";
	}
	end;

OnTimer3960000:
	killmonster "mid_camp","#monster_master::OnMyMobDead";
	mapannounce "mid_camp", "Breeder Taab: I've captured an escaped creature safely. Sorry for yelling so loud.",bc_map,"0x00ff00";
	donpcevent "Hillsrion#alba01::OnEnable";
	donpcevent "Tatacho#alba02::OnEnable";
	donpcevent "Cornus#alba03::OnEnable";
	stopnpctimer;
	end;

OnMyMobDead:
	mapannounce "mid_camp", "Breeder Taab: I've captured an escaped creature safely. Sorry for yelling so loud.",bc_map,"0x00ff00";
	donpcevent "Hillsrion#alba01::OnEnable";
	donpcevent "Tatacho#alba02::OnEnable";
	donpcevent "Cornus#alba03::OnEnable";
	killmonster "mid_camp","#monster_master::OnMyMobDead";
	stopnpctimer;
	end;
}

function Catwarp {
	if (Zeny < getarg(0)) {
		mes "[Cat Paw Agent]";
		mes "别拿钱开玩笑。";
		close;
	}
	close2;
	set Zeny, Zeny-getarg(0);
	switch(getarg(1)) {
		case 1: warp "alberta",117,56; end;
		case 2: warp "prontera",116,72; end;
		case 3: if (checkre(0)) warp "izlude",128,98; else warp "izlude",91,105; end;
		case 4: warp "geffen",120,39; end;
		case 5: warp "payon",161,58; end;
		case 6: warp "morocc",156,46; end;
		case 7: warp "aldebaran",168,112; end;
		case 8: warp "yuno",158,125; end;
		case 9: warp "einbroch",158,301; end;
		case 10: warp "lighthalzen",163,64; end;
		case 11: warp "spl_fild02",32,225; end;
		case 12: warp "man_fild02",129,61; end;
	}
}
}

	next;
	mes "[Expedition Scout]";
	mes "伊格丽德教官命令我寻找某样东西。";
	set .@playtime,checkquest(3091,PLAYTIME);
	next;
	switch(select("询问搜索结果。:闲聊。:离开。")) {
	case 1:
		if (ep13_1_edq == 71 || ep13_1_edq == 72) {
			mes "[Expedition Scout]";
			mes "哦，你一定是"+strcharinfo(0)+"。听说你要递送报告。";
			next;
			if (.@playtime == 0 || .@playtime == 1) {
				mes "[Expedition Scout]";
				mes "寻找遗失的页面比想象中困难。完成一轮后再回来。";
				close;
			} else {
				switch(rand(1,6)) {
				case 1:
				case 6:
					set .@i,2;
					break;
				case 3:
					set .@i,3;
					break;
				case 4:
					set .@i,5;
					break;
				case 2:
				case 5:
					set .@i,0;
					break;
				}
				mes "[Expedition Scout]";
				if (.@i) {
					mes "好消息！我找到了"+.@i+"张似乎属于报告的纸。";
					next;
					getitem 6040,.@i; //Some_Of_Report
				} else {
					mes "我已经尽力了，但还没找到任何页面。";
					next;
				}
				if (.@playtime == -1) {
					changequest 3090,3091;
				} else {
					erasequest 3091;
					setquest 3091;
				}
				mes "[Expedition Scout]";
				mes "那么，继续努力！";
				close;
			}
		}
		mes "[Expedition Scout]";
		mes "你是谁？为什么想知道搜索结果？你是军队的人吗？";
		next;
		mes "[Expedition Scout]";
		mes "喂，打断军事行动可是会被逮捕的，你不知道吗？";
		next;
		mes "[Expedition Scout]";
		mes "请立即离开。";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[Expedition Scout]";
		mes "抱歉，我正在执勤。下班后再聊吧？";
		close;
	case 3:
		close;
	}
}

	donpcevent "ep13_spl_fild02_mon_edq::OnEnable";
	end;

	monster "spl_fild02",0,0,"Luciola Vespa",1994,7,"ep13_spl_fild02_mon_edq::OnMyMobDead";
	monster "spl_fild02",0,0,"Pinguicula",1995,7,"ep13_spl_fild02_mon_edq::OnMyMobDead";
	initnpctimer;
	end;

OnDisable:
	stopnpctimer;
	killmonster "spl_fild02","ep13_spl_fild02_mon_edq::OnMyMobDead";
	disablenpc "ep13_spl_fild02_mon_edq";
	end;

	killmonster "spl_fild02","ep13_spl_fild02_mon_edq::OnMyMobDead";
	stopnpctimer;
	donpcevent "ep13_spl_fild02_mon_edq::OnEnable";
	end;

OnMyMobDead:
	if (mobcount("spl_fild02","ep13_spl_fild02_mon_edq::OnMyMobDead") < 14 && (ep13_1_edq == 71 || ep13_1_edq == 72))
		getitem 6040,1; //Some_Of_Report
	end;
}

		mes "你最好先处理活着的敌人！";
		close;
	}
	end;
